## Reading

The breadth-first run 20261018T132958Z-d2-crash prints the full trace (9 states) of the same violation. Node 1 decides v1 at ballot 11 (state 7). Node 2 enters stage 1 with decided_idx 0 (state 8). In state 9, node 2 serves read 1 from its own log, as stage 1 does: `stale = TRUE`, `result = <<>>`, `decided_at_start = <<v1>>`. A stage-1 read is stale by the decision of the owner. EachReadSeesDecided has no condition `~reads[r].stale`, so it rejects this read. With that condition, the property is the same as R1. The violation is not a fault of the protocol. The property stays unchanged. It is taken out of the cfgs in which a stage-1 read can occur (MC_d0, MC_d2 and its splits) and stays in MC_d1. The owner decides whether the property goes.

Decision of the session after this reading: EachReadSeesDecided gets the condition `~reads[r].stale`, and the property StaleReadIsMarked (a done read whose result does not extend decided_at_start has stale = TRUE) enters the d cfgs (plan item 9). Both runs are repeated.
